commit 6af847ea662cf9f2d4a84bc7d3a05e2a87980f4f
Author: Arnold Bot <bot@example.com>
Date:   Sun Mar 8 22:25:48 2026 +0100

    refactor: share shot lightbox context and timeline preview helpers

diff --git a/src/shared/components/ShotImageManager/hooks/useLightboxContextData.ts b/src/shared/components/ShotImageManager/hooks/useLightboxContextData.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..f84aec0ae
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/shared/components/ShotImageManager/hooks/useLightboxContextData.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,89 @@
+import { useEffect } from 'react';
+import { useTaskDetails } from './useTaskDetails';
+import { usePrefetchTaskData } from '@/shared/hooks/tasks/useTaskPrefetch';
+import { usePendingImageOpen } from '@/shared/hooks/usePendingImageOpen';
+import { useAdjacentSegmentsData } from './useAdjacentSegmentsData';
+import { getGenerationId } from '@/shared/lib/media/mediaTypeHelpers';
+import type { PairData } from '@/shared/types/pairData';
+import type { SegmentSlot } from '@/shared/hooks/segments';
+import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
+
+interface UseLightboxContextDataInput {
+  lightboxIndex: number | null;
+  currentImages: GenerationRow[];
+  setLightboxIndex: (index: number | null) => void;
+  projectId?: string | null;
+  pendingImageToOpen?: string | null;
+  pendingImageVariantId?: string | null;
+  onClearPendingImageToOpen?: () => void;
+  segmentSlots?: SegmentSlot[];
+  onPairClick?: (pairIndex: number, pairData?: PairData) => void;
+  navigateWithTransition?: (doNavigation: () => void) => void;
+}
+
+export function useLightboxContextData({
+  lightboxIndex,
+  currentImages,
+  setLightboxIndex,
+  projectId,
+  pendingImageToOpen,
+  pendingImageVariantId,
+  onClearPendingImageToOpen,
+  segmentSlots,
+  onPairClick,
+  navigateWithTransition,
+}: UseLightboxContextDataInput) {
+  const currentLightboxImage = lightboxIndex !== null ? currentImages[lightboxIndex] : null;
+  const currentLightboxImageId = getGenerationId(currentLightboxImage) || null;
+
+  const { taskDetailsData } = useTaskDetails({
+    generationId: currentLightboxImageId,
+    projectId: projectId ?? null,
+  });
+
+  const prefetchTaskData = usePrefetchTaskData();
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (lightboxIndex === null) return;
+
+    if (lightboxIndex > 0) {
+      const prevGenerationId = getGenerationId(currentImages[lightboxIndex - 1]);
+      if (prevGenerationId) {
+        prefetchTaskData(prevGenerationId);
+      }
+    }
+
+    if (lightboxIndex < currentImages.length - 1) {
+      const nextGenerationId = getGenerationId(currentImages[lightboxIndex + 1]);
+      if (nextGenerationId) {
+        prefetchTaskData(nextGenerationId);
+      }
+    }
+
+    if (currentLightboxImageId) {
+      prefetchTaskData(currentLightboxImageId);
+    }
+  }, [lightboxIndex, currentImages, currentLightboxImageId, prefetchTaskData]);
+
+  const capturedVariantIdRef = usePendingImageOpen({
+    pendingImageToOpen,
+    pendingImageVariantId,
+    images: currentImages,
+    openLightbox: setLightboxIndex,
+    onClear: onClearPendingImageToOpen,
+  });
+
+  const adjacentSegmentsData = useAdjacentSegmentsData({
+    segmentSlots,
+    onPairClick,
+    lightboxIndex,
+    currentImages,
+    setLightboxIndex,
+    navigateWithTransition,
+  });
+
+  return {
+    capturedVariantIdRef,
+    adjacentSegmentsData,
+    taskDetailsData,
+  };
+}
```

diff --git a/src/shared/components/VideoPortionTimeline/hooks/windowDragListeners.ts b/src/shared/components/VideoPortionTimeline/hooks/windowDragListeners.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..713a05e9a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/shared/components/VideoPortionTimeline/hooks/windowDragListeners.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,18 @@
+export function registerWindowDragListeners(
+  handleMove: (event: MouseEvent | TouchEvent) => void,
+  handleEnd: () => void
+): () => void {
+  window.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMove);
+  window.addEventListener('mouseup', handleEnd);
+  window.addEventListener('touchmove', handleMove, { passive: false });
+  window.addEventListener('touchend', handleEnd);
+  window.addEventListener('touchcancel', handleEnd);
+
+  return () => {
+    window.removeEventListener('mousemove', handleMove);
+    window.removeEventListener('mouseup', handleEnd);
+    window.removeEventListener('touchmove', handleMove);
+    window.removeEventListener('touchend', handleEnd);
+    window.removeEventListener('touchcancel', handleEnd);
+  };
+}
```

diff --git a/src/shared/lib/storagePaths.ts b/src/shared/lib/storagePaths.ts
index 846f7434a..7bb813749 100644
--- a/src/shared/lib/storagePaths.ts
+++ b/src/shared/lib/storagePaths.ts
@@ -14,18 +14,43 @@ export function generateThumbnailFilename(): string {
   return `thumb_${Date.now()}_${randomToken(6)}.jpg`;
 }
 
+function extensionFromFilename(filename: string): string | null {
+  const normalized = filename.trim();
+  if (!normalized) {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  const filePart = normalized.split(/[\\/]/).pop() ?? normalized;
+  const sanitizedFilePart = filePart.split(/[?#]/, 1)[0];
+  const extensionMatch = /\.([a-zA-Z0-9]+)$/.exec(sanitizedFilePart);
+  if (!extensionMatch?.[1]) {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  return extensionMatch[1].toLowerCase();
+}
+
+function extensionFromMimeType(mimeType: string): string | null {
+  const subtype = mimeType.split('/')[1]?.split(';')[0]?.trim().toLowerCase();
+  if (!subtype) {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  return subtype === 'jpeg' ? 'jpg' : subtype;
+}
+
 export function getFileExtension(
   filename: string,
   mimeType?: string,
   defaultExt: string = 'bin'
 ): string {
-  const parts = filename.split('.');
-  if (parts.length > 1) {
-    return parts.pop()!;
+  const filenameExtension = extensionFromFilename(filename);
+  if (filenameExtension) {
+    return filenameExtension;
   }
 
   if (mimeType) {
-    const mimeExt = mimeType.split('/')[1]?.replace('jpeg', 'jpg');
+    const mimeExt = extensionFromMimeType(mimeType);
     if (mimeExt) return mimeExt;
   }

+  inputImages,
+  detailsVariant,
+  isMobile,
+  availableLoras,
+  controls,
+  showCopyButtons,
+  children,
+}: TaskDetailsSummarySectionProps) {
+  return (
+    <TaskDetailsSummaryAndParams
+      task={task}
+      inputImages={inputImages}
+      detailsVariant={detailsVariant}
+      isMobile={isMobile}
+      availableLoras={availableLoras}
+      showAllImages={controls.showAllImages}
+      onShowAllImagesChange={controls.onShowAllImagesChange}
+      showFullPrompt={controls.showFullPrompt}
+      onShowFullPromptChange={controls.onShowFullPromptChange}
+      showFullNegativePrompt={controls.showFullNegativePrompt}
+      onShowFullNegativePromptChange={controls.onShowFullNegativePromptChange}
+      showDetailedParams={controls.showDetailedParams}
+      onShowDetailedParamsChange={controls.onShowDetailedParamsChange}
+      paramsCopied={controls.paramsCopied}
+      onCopyParams={controls.onCopyParams}
+      showCopyButtons={showCopyButtons}
+    >
+      {children}
+    </TaskDetailsSummaryAndParams>
+  );
+}
```

Now I have enough information to give a thorough review. Here is my assessment of all 30 commits:

---

## Commit-by-commit review

### 80d53c55b - share onboarding and segment/shot manager prop contracts
**QUESTIONABLE.** Mixed bag. The `OnboardingContinueButton` / `OnboardingStepWithContinue` extractions are trivial (a button in a div with a chevron icon) -- this is the kind of thing that's fine inline. However, the `SegmentSettingsForm/types.ts` changes are good: exporting previously-private interfaces that were being re-declared in child components, and extracting shared prop shapes like `SegmentFieldSectionProps`. Net ~43 lines removed, but the onboarding part is over-extraction for 2 consumers.

### 6af847ea6 - share shot lightbox context and timeline preview helpers
**GOOD.** `useLightboxContextData` consolidates ~70 lines of identical task-detail prefetching, pending-image-open, and adjacent-segment logic that was copy-pasted between `ShotImageManagerDesktop` and `ShotImageManagerMobileWrapper`. 3 consumers, -175 net lines. Clean extraction of genuinely duplicated hook orchestration.

### 356dd5be9 - share pair tooltip, scrubbing, and task-detail primitives
**QUESTIONABLE.** This commit tries to do too many things at once (5 new files). The good parts: `useSegmentScrubbingCore` (94 lines, 3 consumers) consolidates real scrubbing logic. The questionable parts: `derivedNavigation.ts` has only 2 consumers and is a ~40-line pure function that could stay inline. `TaskDetailsField` (25 lines, simple label+value div) and `TaskDetailsImageBlock` (38 lines, label+img) are very thin wrappers -- the duplication they remove is trivial string interpolation (`config.textSize`, `config.fontWeight`). These are the kind of "share a 3-line pattern" extractions that add import indirection without meaningful dedup.

### 9aae55257 - share task-details state blocks and selector contracts
**QUESTIONABLE / BAD in parts.** This is a big commit (+528/-280) that creates 6 new files. The problematic pieces:
- `TaskDetailsSummarySection` (62 lines) is a **pure property-forwarding wrapper** around `TaskDetailsSummaryAndParams`. It takes a `controls` object and destructures it into individual props. This is the exact `lightboxRenderBuilders` anti-pattern that was already reverted. It doesn't reduce any logic -- it just re-packages props.
- `taskDetailsLayout.ts` (20 lines) is a single function `getTaskDetailsCardClassName` that does `cn(baseClassName, conditional)`. This is a 3-line function that doesn't justify a separate file.
- `variantCardSharedTypes.ts` -- type-only extraction with 2 consumers, ~25 props. Fine for reducing prop-type duplication between VariantCard and VariantGrid.
- `TaskDetailsStatusStates` (112 lines, loading/error/empty) and `TaskDetailsLazyVideoPreview` (101 lines) -- these are reasonable component extractions used across multiple task detail views (~12 consumers total for the group). The status states are genuinely repeated.
- `TaskDetailsEnhancementSection` -- reasonable, used by multiple task detail components.

Net assessment: the status states and enhancement section are fine, but `TaskDetailsSummarySection` is prop-forwarding boilerplate and `taskDetailsLayout.ts` is too trivial for its own file.

### b82c45a6a - share drag listeners and text field action primitives
**QUESTIONABLE.** Another multi-concern commit. The `textFieldActions.tsx` extraction (167 lines) to deduplicate voice input / clear button logic between `input.tsx` and `textarea.tsx` is reasonable -- that was genuine duplication. However:
- `buttonPrimitive.tsx` (16 lines) -- a component that does `const Comp = asChild ? Slot : 'button'` and passes `cn(className)`. Only 2 consumers. This is too thin.
- `windowDragListeners.ts` (18 lines) -- registers/unregisters 5 window event listeners. Only 2 consumers (both in `VideoPortionTimeline/hooks`). Could stay as a local helper.
- `toJsonObject.ts` (8 lines) -- a cast-to-record function. 4 consumers, but **there's already `toObjectRecord` in `src/shared/lib/jsonRecord.ts`** doing nearly the same thing (created in the same batch!). These should be one function, not two.

### a57187b1a - demote non-fatal edge logging and theme chart colors
**GOOD.** Changes `console.error` to `console.warn` for non-fatal conditions in `complete_task`. Small, targeted, correct.

### e21a1bea8 - type media gallery lightbox session hook contracts
**GOOD.** Replaces 12 `any` types with proper typed interfaces derived from `MediaGalleryLightboxSession`. No new files, no indirection -- just better types.

### a61af3b98 - remove explicit any from timeline track content props
**GOOD.** Single-line `any` to proper type. Clean.

### 8940bc4ea - make complete_task catch flows explicit
**GOOD.** Adds explicit error typing in catch blocks. Small, correct.

### 2bf22d7d3 - remove hardcoded URL literals from queue targets
**GOOD.** Moves test URLs to config. Small, sensible.

### 45920921e - drop dead exports across shared modules
**GOOD.** Removes unused exports across 58 files. Changes `export function` to `function` and `export interface` to `interface` for things only used locally. This is pure cleanup with no new abstractions.

### 59ca3b1d3 - replace ts-expect-error in huggingface upload
**GOOD.** Replaces `@ts-expect-error` with a proper typed dynamic import + interface declaration. This is the right way to handle a Deno edge function importing an ESM package without types. +28 lines but they're all type declarations and a clean dynamic import pattern.

### 501871ca8 - decouple shared edit-video defaults and clear unuseds
**GOOD.** Moves shared `editVideoDefaults` from tool-specific to shared config, removes unused imports. Net -1 line, cleaner layering.

### 05ea3ad10 - share project cache and JSON record helpers
**QUESTIONABLE.** `jsonRecord.ts` creates `toObjectRecord` (7 lines) -- a trivial cast function. Then commit `b82c45a6a` later creates the near-identical `toJsonObject`. These two helpers do the same thing with slightly different return types and null-vs-empty-object semantics. The `ProjectScopedCache.getProjectWithFallback` method addition is fine.

### 36d80a746 - share task project scope resolution
**GOOD.** `resolveTaskProjectScope` (14 lines, 4 consumers) consolidates identical "determine project-scoped vs all-tasks query key" logic from 3 call sites. Net -19 lines. Clean.

### 73e39172f - centralize shots list invalidation helper
**GOOD.** Tiny `cacheUtils.ts` (7 lines) to avoid repeating the same query key invalidation pattern. Net -4 lines but removes duplication in a correctness-sensitive area.

### ecd4c8985 - share shot image selector logic
**GOOD.** `shotImageSelectors.ts` (43 lines, 2 consumers) extracts timeline/unpositioned/video filtering logic that was duplicated between `useShotImages` and `useShotEditorSetup`. These are non-trivial filter predicates with actual logic. Net -23 lines.

### e8e706dd5 - share upload xhr lifecycle across media uploaders
**GOOD.** `uploadXhrLifecycle.ts` (44 lines, 2 consumers) deduplicates the identical stall-check/timeout/progress-tracking lifecycle between `imageUploader` and `videoUploader`. Net -20 lines of genuinely duplicated async management logic.

### a2bd8c1cd - share media frame capture and auth primitives
**QUESTIONABLE.** `videoFrameCapturePrimitives.ts` (27 lines, 2 consumers) extracts `createElement('video')` + `createElement('canvas')` + `getContext('2d')` into a shared function. The functions are very small: `createVideoFrameCaptureElements` is 6 lines of DOM calls, `configureVideoForFrameCapture` is 5 lines of property assignments. This is borderline too granular for extraction.

### d4cbb02bb - normalize operation result constructors
**GOOD.** Single-file refactor of `operationResult.ts`. Extracts `normalizeOperationError` helper and `DEFAULT_FAILURE_POLICY` constant. Makes the constructors more consistent. No new files, no new indirection.

### a32e60cec - share created-at sorting comparators
**GOOD.** `createdAtSort.ts` (16 lines, 4 consumers including the follow-up commit). Simple, useful, and the `HasCreatedAt` interface handles both `created_at` and `createdAt` naming conventions cleanly.

### 48caa32a9 - reuse created-at comparator in segment sorting
**GOOD.** Follow-up that wires existing consumers to the new comparator. Net -7 lines. Clean.

### bf933deb4 - share variant badge cache updates
**GOOD.** `variantBadgeCacheUtils.ts` (24 lines, 2 consumers) consolidates cache update logic between `useMarkVariantViewed` and `useVariantBadges`. Net -6 lines but removes duplication in cache-manipulation logic where consistency matters.

### 71dca780f - reuse shared record coercion in task params
**GOOD.** Wires `taskParamContract.ts` to use existing `toObjectRecord`. Net -5 lines.

### ba6be3715 - reuse realtime error policy helpers
**GOOD.** Removes 51 lines of duplicated error policy logic from `DataFreshnessManager` by reusing existing helpers. Net -45 lines. Excellent.

### 52ec8fb97 - harden shared file extension parsing
**GOOD.** Replaces naive `filename.split('.').pop()` with robust parsing that handles paths, query strings, hash fragments. +25 net lines but fixes real edge cases (URLs with query params, backslash paths).

### 5fa14f741 - resolve stale async_no_await queue cluster
**QUESTIONABLE.** Empty commit (no file changes). Presumably this resolved some queue item but the commit is a no-op.

### 2032b9a96 - share join-clips generation success feedback
**GOOD.** Uses existing `flashSuccessForDuration` and `invalidateTaskAndProjectQueries` helpers. Small, net 0 new code.

### 63bb612aa - share character animate media panel shell
**QUESTIONABLE.** `MediaPanelShared.tsx` (77 lines, 2 consumers) extracts `MediaDropZone`, `DeleteMediaButton`, and `ReplaceDropOverlay` from `CharacterImagePanel` and `MotionVideoPanel`. These are within the same tool (`character-animate`), not cross-cutting. The drop zone is reasonable to share, but `DeleteMediaButton` is 15 lines and `ReplaceDropOverlay` is 8 lines -- those are too trivial. Net +47 lines.

### effe01dba - share upload validation and video gallery defaults
**QUESTIONABLE.** Two extractions:
- `fileValidation.ts` (25 lines, 2 consumers, both within `character-animate`) -- `getFirstFile`, `isSupportedImageType`, `isSupportedVideoType` are 1-2 line functions. These are too granular for extraction, especially within a single tool.
- `videoGalleryDefaults.ts` (27 lines, 2 consumers) -- consolidates video gallery config defaults. Reasonable.

## Summary

**GOOD (17):** `6af847ea6`, `a57187b1a`, `e21a1bea8`, `a61af3b98`, `8940bc4ea`, `2bf22d7d3`, `45920921e`, `59ca3b1d3`, `501871ca8`, `36d80a746`, `73e39172f`, `ecd4c8985`, `e8e706dd5`, `d4cbb02bb`, `a32e60cec`, `48caa32a9`, `bf933deb4`, `71dca780f`, `ba6be3715`, `52ec8fb97`, `2032b9a96`

**QUESTIONABLE (10):** `80d53c55b`, `356dd5be9`, `b82c45a6a`, `05ea3ad10`, `a2bd8c1cd`, `5fa14f741`, `63bb612aa`, `effe01dba`, `9aae55257`

**BAD (0):** None are outright bad, but parts of `9aae55257` come close.

## Top issues to address

1. **Duplicate JSON record helpers**: `src/shared/lib/jsonRecord.ts` (`toObjectRecord`) and `src/shared/lib/json/toJsonObject.ts` (`toJsonObject`) do the same thing. Consolidate into one.

2. **`TaskDetailsSummarySection` is a prop-forwarding wrapper** (`9aae55257`): It takes a `controls` object and passes each field as an individual prop to `TaskDetailsSummaryAndParams`. This is the same anti-pattern as `lightboxRenderBuilders`. Either the underlying component should accept the `controls` object directly, or the wrapper should be removed.

3. **`taskDetailsLayout.ts` is too trivial** (`9aae55257`): A single 4-line function in its own file. Inline it or merge into an adjacent file.

4. **Micro-extractions within `character-animate`** (`effe01dba`, `63bb612aa`): `getFirstFile` (1 line), `isSupportedImageType` (1 line), `DeleteMediaButton` (15 lines), `ReplaceDropOverlay` (8 lines) -- these are not cross-cutting and are too small for their own shared modules. They should stay as local helpers within their consuming files.

5. **`buttonPrimitive.tsx`** (`b82c45a6a`): A 16-line component that just conditionally renders `Slot` or `button`. Only 2 consumers.

6. **`videoFrameCapturePrimitives.ts`** (`a2bd8c1cd`): Wrapping `document.createElement('video')` + `document.createElement('canvas')` in a shared function is borderline too granular. The 2 consumers could each do these 5 lines inline.

7. **Empty commit** `5fa14f741`: No file changes. Should be squashed or removed.

new file mode 100644
index 000000000..c248ad842
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/settings/inheritedDefaults.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,38 @@
+import { useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
+import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
+
+interface UseInheritedDefaultsOptions<T> {
+  shotId: string | null | undefined;
+  storageKeyForShot: (shotId: string) => string;
+  mergeDefaults: (defaults: Record<string, unknown>) => T;
+  context: string;
+}
+
+export function useSessionInheritedDefaults<T>({
+  shotId,
+  storageKeyForShot,
+  mergeDefaults,
+  context,
+}: UseInheritedDefaultsOptions<T>): T | null {
+  const appliedShotRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
+
+  return useMemo(() => {
+    if (!shotId || typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
+    if (appliedShotRef.current === shotId) return null;
+
+    const [REDACTED](shotId);
+    const rawDefaults = sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey);
+    if (!rawDefaults) return null;
+
+    try {
+      const parsedDefaults = JSON.parse(rawDefaults) as Record<string, unknown>;
+      sessionStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
+      appliedShotRef.current = shotId;
+      return mergeDefaults(parsedDefaults);
+    } catch (error) {
+      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context, showToast: false });
+      sessionStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
+      return null;
+    }
+  }, [shotId, storageKeyForShot, mergeDefaults, context]);
+}

Author: Arnold Bot <bot@example.com>
Date:   Mon Mar 9 00:35:11 2026 +0100

    refactor: share join clips frame scaling helper

diff --git a/src/shared/lib/joinClipsFrameScaling.ts b/src/shared/lib/joinClipsFrameScaling.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..24e332344
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/shared/lib/joinClipsFrameScaling.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,31 @@
+interface ScaleJoinFrameCountsInput {
+  contextFrameCount: number;
+  gapFrameCount: number;
+  shortestClipFrames: number | null | undefined;
+}
+
+interface ScaleJoinFrameCountsOutput {
+  contextFrameCount: number;
+  gapFrameCount: number;
+}
+
+export function scaleJoinFrameCountsToShortestClip({
+  contextFrameCount,
+  gapFrameCount,
+  shortestClipFrames,
+}: ScaleJoinFrameCountsInput): ScaleJoinFrameCountsOutput {
+  if (!shortestClipFrames || shortestClipFrames <= 0) {
+    return { contextFrameCount, gapFrameCount };
+  }
+
+  const framesNeeded = gapFrameCount + 2 * contextFrameCount;
+  if (framesNeeded <= shortestClipFrames) {
+    return { contextFrameCount, gapFrameCount };
+  }
+
+  const scale = shortestClipFrames / framesNeeded;
+  return {
+    contextFrameCount: Math.max(4, Math.floor(contextFrameCount * scale)),
+    gapFrameCount: Math.max(1, Math.floor(gapFrameCount * scale)),
+  };
+}

commit 4bf30b2c1d3c02ad230cebd2b9e36f67c6a75230
Author: Arnold Bot <bot@example.com>
Date:   Mon Mar 9 00:32:53 2026 +0100

    refactor: share raf sticky observer primitives

diff --git a/src/shared/lib/sticky/rafStickyObserver.ts b/src/shared/lib/sticky/rafStickyObserver.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..96d243b44
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/shared/lib/sticky/rafStickyObserver.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,69 @@
+interface RafStickyObserverOptions {
+  element: HTMLElement;
+  isStickyRef: { current: boolean };
+  setSticky: (value: boolean) => void;
+  computeThresholdY: () => number;
+  shouldRunInitialCheck?: boolean;
+}
+
+export function getDocumentScrollTop(): number {
+  return window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop || 0;
+}
+
+export function getElementDocumentTop(element: Element): number {
+  const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
+  return rect.top + getDocumentScrollTop();
+}
+
+export function attachRafStickyObserver({
+  element,
+  isStickyRef,
+  setSticky,
+  computeThresholdY,
+  shouldRunInitialCheck = true,
+}: RafStickyObserverOptions): () => void {
+  const stickyThresholdY = { current: 0 };
+  let rafId: number | null = null;
+
+  const checkSticky = () => {
+    rafId = null;
+    const shouldBeSticky = getDocumentScrollTop() > stickyThresholdY.current;
+    if (shouldBeSticky !== isStickyRef.current) {
+      isStickyRef.current = shouldBeSticky;
+      setSticky(shouldBeSticky);
+    }
+  };
+
+  const onScroll = () => {
+    if (rafId !== null) return;
+    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(checkSticky);
+  };
+
+  const onResize = () => {
+    stickyThresholdY.current = computeThresholdY();
+    if (rafId !== null) {
+      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
+    }
+    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(checkSticky);
+  };
+
+  stickyThresholdY.current = computeThresholdY();
+  if (shouldRunInitialCheck) {
+    checkSticky();
+  }
+
+  window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
+  window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
+
+  const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => onResize());
+  resizeObserver.observe(element);
+
+  return () => {
+    window.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll);
+    window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
+    if (rafId !== null) {
+      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
+    }
+    resizeObserver.disconnect();
+  };
+}

diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/autoTopupRequest.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/autoTopupRequest.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..0f498acd2
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/autoTopupRequest.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,21 @@
+import { createStripeClient } from "./autoTopupDomain.ts";
+import { jsonResponse } from "./http.ts";
+
+interface AutoTopupLogger {
+  error: (message: string, context?: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
+  flush: () => Promise<void>;
+}
+
+export async function ensureStripeClient(logger: AutoTopupLogger): Promise<
+  | { ok: true; stripe: ReturnType<typeof createStripeClient> extends { ok: true; value: infer T } ? T : never }
+  | { ok: false; response: Response }
+> {
+  const stripeResult = createStripeClient();
+  if (!stripeResult.ok) {
+    logger.error(stripeResult.error.logMessage);
+    await logger.flush();
+    return { ok: false, response: jsonResponse({ error: stripeResult.error.message }, 500) };
+  }
+
+  return { ok: true, stripe: stripeResult.value };
+}
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/requestGuards.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/requestGuards.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..cfae025fd
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/requestGuards.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
+import { jsonResponse } from "./http.ts";
+
+interface GuardAuth {
+  isServiceRole: boolean;
+  userId: string | null;
+}
+
+interface GuardLogger {
+  error: (message: string, context?: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
+}
+
+interface UserGuardResult {
+  ok: true;
+  userId: string;
+}
+
+interface ServiceGuardResult {
+  ok: true;
+}
+
+interface GuardFailure {
+  ok: false;
+  response: Response;
+}
+
+type GuardResult<T> = T | GuardFailure;
+
+export const JWT_AUTH_REQUIRED = {
+  required: true,
+  options: { allowJwtUserAuth: true },
+} as const;
+
+export function authenticationFailedResponse(logger: GuardLogger): Response {
+  logger.error("Authentication failed");
+  return jsonResponse({ error: "Authentication failed" }, 401);
+}
+
+export function ensureTaskActor(
+  auth: GuardAuth | null | undefined,
+  logger: GuardLogger,
+): GuardResult<{ ok: true }> {
+  if (auth && (auth.userId || auth.isServiceRole)) {
+    return { ok: true };
+  }
+  return { ok: false, response: authenticationFailedResponse(logger) };
+}
+
+export function ensureUserAuth(
+  auth: GuardAuth | null | undefined,
+  logger: GuardLogger,
+): GuardResult<UserGuardResult> {
+  if (auth?.userId) {
+    return { ok: true, userId: auth.userId };
+  }
+  return { ok: false, response: authenticationFailedResponse(logger) };
+}
+
+export function ensureServiceRoleAuth(
+  auth: GuardAuth | null | undefined,
+  logger: GuardLogger,
+): GuardResult<ServiceGuardResult> {
+  if (auth?.isServiceRole) {
+    return { ok: true };
+  }
+  return { ok: false, response: authenticationFailedResponse(logger) };
+}
+
+export function normalizeTaskId(value: unknown): string {
+  if (typeof value === "string" || typeof value === "number") {
+    return String(value);
+  }
+  return "";
+}

diff --git a/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/sections/generation/joinClipsFormProps.ts b/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/sections/generation/joinClipsFormProps.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..2b25c4ebe
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/sections/generation/joinClipsFormProps.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,86 @@
+import type React from 'react';
+import type { LoraModel } from '@/domains/lora/types/lora';
+import type { UseLoraManagerReturn } from '@/domains/lora/hooks/useLoraManager';
+import type { JoinSegmentsSettings } from '@/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/settings/useJoinSegmentsSettings';
+import {
+  JoinClipsSettingsForm,
+  DEFAULT_JOIN_CLIPS_PHASE_CONFIG,
+  BUILTIN_JOIN_CLIPS_DEFAULT_ID,
+} from '@/shared/components/JoinClipsSettingsForm/JoinClipsSettingsForm';
+
+interface JoinFormSettingsState {
+  settings: JoinSegmentsSettings;
+  updateField: <K extends keyof JoinSegmentsSettings>(field: K, value: JoinSegmentsSettings[K]) => void;
+  updateFields: (fields: Partial<JoinSegmentsSettings>) => void;
+}
+
+interface JoinFormJoinState {
+  joinSettings: JoinFormSettingsState;
+  joinLoraManager: UseLoraManagerReturn;
+  joinValidationData: {
+    shortestClipFrames?: number;
+  };
+}
+
+interface BuildJoinClipsFormPropsInput {
+  joinState: JoinFormJoinState;
+  availableLoras: LoraModel[];
+  projectId: string;
+  loraPersistenceKey: string;
+}
+
+interface BuildJoinClipsFormPropsResult {
+  clipSettings: React.ComponentProps<typeof JoinClipsSettingsForm>['clipSettings'];
+  motionConfig: React.ComponentProps<typeof JoinClipsSettingsForm>['motionConfig'];
+}
+
+export function buildJoinClipsFormProps({
+  joinState,
+  availableLoras,
+  projectId,
+  loraPersistenceKey,
+}: BuildJoinClipsFormPropsInput): BuildJoinClipsFormPropsResult {
+  const joinSettings = joinState.joinSettings.settings;
+
+  return {
+    clipSettings: {
+      gapFrames: joinSettings.gapFrameCount,
+      setGapFrames: (val) => joinState.joinSettings.updateField('gapFrameCount', val),
+      contextFrames: joinSettings.contextFrameCount,
+      setContextFrames: (val) => joinState.joinSettings.updateField('contextFrameCount', val),
+      replaceMode: joinSettings.replaceMode,
+      setReplaceMode: (val) => joinState.joinSettings.updateField('replaceMode', val),
+      keepBridgingImages: joinSettings.keepBridgingImages,
+      setKeepBridgingImages: (val) => joinState.joinSettings.updateField('keepBridgingImages', val),
+      prompt: joinSettings.prompt,
+      setPrompt: (val) => joinState.joinSettings.updateField('prompt', val),
+      negativePrompt: joinSettings.negativePrompt,
+      setNegativePrompt: (val) => joinState.joinSettings.updateField('negativePrompt', val),
+      enhancePrompt: joinSettings.enhancePrompt,
+      setEnhancePrompt: (val) => joinState.joinSettings.updateField('enhancePrompt', val),
+      shortestClipFrames: joinState.joinValidationData.shortestClipFrames,
+    },
+    motionConfig: {
+      availableLoras,
+      projectId,
+      loraPersistenceKey,
+      loraManager: joinState.joinLoraManager,
+      motionMode: joinSettings.motionMode,
+      onMotionModeChange: (mode) => joinState.joinSettings.updateField('motionMode', mode),
+      phaseConfig: joinSettings.phaseConfig ?? DEFAULT_JOIN_CLIPS_PHASE_CONFIG,
+      onPhaseConfigChange: (config) => joinState.joinSettings.updateField('phaseConfig', config),
+      randomSeed: joinSettings.randomSeed,
+      onRandomSeedChange: (val) => joinState.joinSettings.updateField('randomSeed', val),
+      selectedPhasePresetId: joinSettings.selectedPhasePresetId ?? BUILTIN_JOIN_CLIPS_DEFAULT_ID,
+      onPhasePresetSelect: (presetId, config) => {
+        joinState.joinSettings.updateFields({
+          selectedPhasePresetId: presetId,
+          phaseConfig: config,
+        });
+      },
+      onPhasePresetRemove: () => {
+        joinState.joinSettings.updateField('selectedPhasePresetId', null);
+      },
+    },
+  };
+}
```

new file mode 100644
index 000000000..8aff1fcff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/tools/training-data-helper/components/BatchDialogFormFields.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,71 @@
+import { Button } from '@/shared/components/ui/button';
+import { Input } from '@/shared/components/ui/input';
+import { Label } from '@/shared/components/ui/primitives/label';
+import { Textarea } from '@/shared/components/ui/textarea';
+
+interface BatchDialogFormFieldsProps {
+  nameId: string;
+  nameValue: string;
+  onNameChange: (value: string) => void;
+  descriptionId: string;
+  descriptionValue: string;
+  onDescriptionChange: (value: string) => void;
+  isSubmitting: boolean;
+  submitLabel: string;
+  submittingLabel: string;
+  onCancel: () => void;
+  onSubmit: () => void;
+}
+
+export function BatchDialogFormFields({
+  nameId,
+  nameValue,
+  onNameChange,
+  descriptionId,
+  descriptionValue,
+  onDescriptionChange,
+  isSubmitting,
+  submitLabel,
+  submittingLabel,
+  onCancel,
+  onSubmit,
+}: BatchDialogFormFieldsProps) {
+  return (
+    <div className="space-y-4">
+      <div>
+        <Label htmlFor={nameId}>Batch Name</Label>
+        <Input
+          id={nameId}
+          value={nameValue}
+          onChange={(event) => onNameChange(event.target.value)}
+          placeholder="Enter batch name..."
+        />
+      </div>
+      <div>
+        <Label htmlFor={descriptionId}>Description (optional)</Label>
+        <Textarea
+          id={descriptionId}
+          value={descriptionValue}
+          onChange={(event) => onDescriptionChange(event.target.value)}
+          placeholder="Describe this batch..."
+          rows={3}
+        />
+      </div>
+      <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
+        <Button
+          variant="outline"
+          onClick={onCancel}
+          disabled={isSubmitting}
+        >
+          Cancel
+        </Button>
+        <Button
+          onClick={onSubmit}
+          disabled={!nameValue.trim() || isSubmitting}
+        >
+          {isSubmitting ? submittingLabel : submitLabel}
+        </Button>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  );
+}

diff --git a/src/tools/training-data-helper/lib/videoFormatters.ts b/src/tools/training-data-helper/lib/videoFormatters.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..0426eab78
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/tools/training-data-helper/lib/videoFormatters.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+const BYTES_PER_KB = 1024;
+
+export function formatDurationMinutesSeconds(seconds: number): string {
+  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
+  const remainingSeconds = Math.floor(seconds % 60);
+  return `${minutes}:${remainingSeconds.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
+}
+
+export function formatFileSize(bytes: number): string {
+  if (bytes === 0) return '0 Bytes';
+
+  const sizes = ['Bytes', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
+  const unitIndex = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(BYTES_PER_KB));
+  return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(BYTES_PER_KB, unitIndex)).toFixed(2)) + ' ' + sizes[unitIndex];
+}

---

Here is the review of all 15 commits:

### 1. `4bf30b2c1` - share raf sticky observer primitives
**GOOD.** Two identical ~50-line scroll/resize/RAF observer patterns extracted into a shared 69-line helper. Both consumers (`image-generation/hooks/useStickyHeader.ts` and `travel-between-images/hooks/useStickyHeader.ts`) are genuinely simplified. The shared function is behavioral (not property-mapping boilerplate), and the `shouldRunInitialCheck` option handles the one behavioral difference cleanly.

### 2. `3daf9422c` - share join clips frame scaling helper
**GOOD.** Identical 8-line frame-scaling math duplicated in two places (`useJoinClipsGenerate.ts` and `useJoinSegmentsHandler.ts`). The shared helper at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/joinClipsFrameScaling.ts` is a pure function with typed input/output. Clean dedup.

### 3. `80ae51ff2` - share training dialogs and video format utilities
**QUESTIONABLE.** Two parts:
- `BatchDialogFormFields.tsx`: Extracts shared form fields from Create/Edit batch dialogs (2 consumers, both in `training-data-helper`). This is fine -- real UI dedup, ~30 lines of repeated JSX.
- `videoFormatters.ts`: `formatDurationMinutesSeconds` and `formatFileSize` extracted from inline functions in 2 components. Fine, these are genuine duplicates.
- `ShotCardPlaceholderGrid.tsx`: Extracts a 6-line `div` structure used in 2 places. **This is a micro-extraction** -- it's 5 divs wrapped in a fragment. The "component" is just HTML structure with no behavior, no props, no state. Not harmful but adds a file for very little value.

### 4. `4f0248b10` - resolve stale passthrough and single-use findings
**BAD.** Empty commit. Zero file changes. Should not have been committed.

### 5. `a6897d2c0` - normalize shared generation settings layout classes
**QUESTIONABLE.** Extracts 3 Tailwind class strings into module-level constants in a single file (`SharedGenerationView.tsx`). These constants are used once each within the same file. This is just renaming inline strings to variables -- no dedup, no sharing, no behavioral benefit. Adds indirection without value.

### 6. `b09413fbc` - share shot editor header section types
**QUESTIONABLE.** Extracts `HeaderSectionCallbacks` and `HeaderSectionLayout` interfaces into a shared types file. Used by exactly 2 consumers (`ShotEditorLayout.tsx` and `HeaderSection.tsx`), both in the same directory. The types were inline in each file and identical. This is borderline -- it removes duplication of type definitions, but `ShotEditorLayout.tsx` now uses `HeaderSectionCallbacks & HeaderSectionLayout` as a flat intersection, which is a different shape than the original `header: { ... }` object. The flattening of the `header` prop is a subtle API change worth noting.

### 7. `34252f189` - share edge auth guards and stripe bootstrap helpers
**GOOD.** `requestGuards.ts` creates `ensureUserAuth`, `ensureServiceRoleAuth`, `ensureTaskActor`, `normalizeTaskId`, and `JWT_AUTH_REQUIRED`. These replace repeated 3-5 line auth check patterns across 13+ edge functions. `autoTopupRequest.ts` extracts `ensureStripeClient` used by 3 functions. High consumer count, consistent pattern, meaningful dedup. The `grant-credits` error response format change (from plain text to JSON) is a bonus behavioral fix bundled in.

### 8. `9ca1eeaa5` - share travel-between-images ui and drag primitives
**QUESTIONABLE.** This is a 40-file kitchen-sink commit mixing several unrelated changes:
- `joinSegments.types.ts` and `joinClipsFormProps.ts`: Property-mapping / type extraction. `joinClipsFormProps.ts` (86 lines) is a `buildJoinClipsFormProps` function that maps settings fields to component props -- this is the **lightboxRenderBuilders anti-pattern** explicitly called out. It maps `joinSettings.prompt` to `clipSettings.prompt`, `joinSettings.gapFrameCount` to `clipSettings.gapFrames`, etc. Pure property shuffling.
- `positionConflict.ts`, `imageDropValidation.ts`: Small shared helpers within the drag system. Reasonable.
- `PanelSectionHeader.tsx`, `aspectRatio.ts`: New shared components/utilities. Fine but hard to verify value given the commit size.
- `UnpositionedGenerationsBanner.tsx`: Extracted component, used in 1 place based on the diff. Single-consumer extraction.
- `mediaHandlers.ts` type file: New types. Would need to verify consumer count.
- The `useEditingController.ts` change spreads `...joinSetup` instead of destructuring 25+ individual fields. **This is actually good** -- but it removes the explicit contract documentation.

The commit should have been split into 3-4 focused commits. As-is, the property-mapping builder and single-consumer extractions are concerning.

### 9. `94ba594b6` - share settings inheritance defaults hook
**GOOD.** `useSessionInheritedDefaults` extracts a 25-line pattern (check sessionStorage, parse JSON, mark as applied, handle errors) duplicated identically in `useJoinSegmentsSettings` and `useShotSettings`. The shared hook is parameterized cleanly with `storageKeyForShot` and `mergeDefaults` callbacks. Net code reduction with better error handling.

### 10. `d591317eb` - remove unused imports and stale helpers
**QUESTIONABLE.** Mostly good (removing unused imports, deleting dead `parseGenerationMetadata` wrapper, removing `resetToastManagerForTests`). But it **re-exports** `ToastProps`, `ToastActionElement`, and `toGenerationMetadata` (changes them from private to `export`) -- only for `81d3da7a2` to delete them two commits later. This suggests the commit was generated mechanically without checking the pipeline. The unused import removals and the `segmentSettingsMigration.test.ts` cleanup are fine. Also has a double-semicolon artifact (`;;`) left in `MediaGallery.tsx` line ~207 and `PromptGenerationControls.tsx`.

### 11. `93c1abc29` - remove tagged debug logs from complete_task handlers
**BAD.** This removes `console.warn` from error/catch paths in the `complete_task` edge function -- the most critical server-side code path. Specific concerns:
- `findSourceGenerationByImageUrl`: Removes the only logging when a DB query fails or throws. Now silently returns `null` with no diagnostic trace.
- `linkGenerationToShot`: Removes logging when linking fails. Now silently returns with no trace.
- `validateStoragePathSecurity`: Removes the security warning log when a non-orchestrator task attempts cross-task file access. **This is a security audit log** that should not be removed.
- `handleStorageOperations`: Removes upload error logging before throwing. The throw preserves the error, but the log provided context.
- `handleThumbnail`: Removes thumbnail error logging. Returns `null` silently.
- `request.ts` base64 decode errors: Removes logging before returning `badRequest`. The error response preserves the fact that something failed, but loses the actual error details.

Edge functions don't have persistent logging infrastructure -- `console.warn`/`console.error` in Supabase Edge Functions go to the function logs, which is the **only** way to diagnose production issues. These were not "debug logs" -- they were operational error logging.

### 12. `81d3da7a2` - remove dead exports from shared and tool helpers
**GOOD.** Correctly narrows visibility: makes module-internal functions private (`isVideoOutputGeneration`, `isTimelineImageGeneration`, `isUnpositionedImageGeneration`, `getDocumentScrollTop`, `getFirstFile`, `PositionConflictResolution`, `UploadXhrLifecycle`, `VideoFrameCaptureElements`). The 203-line deletion of `generationMetadata.ts` parsing functions is confirmed dead -- grep shows zero consumers. The `ToastProps`/`ToastActionElement` removal is also correct (zero consumers).

### 13. `b1cc99803` - centralize edge alias URL key construction
**BAD.** Replaces readable URL strings like `'https://deno.land/std@0.224.0/http/server.ts'` with template literal constructions like `` [`${DENO_LAND_BASE}/std@0.224.0/http/server.ts`] ``. This is in a test config file. The URLs are static, never change, and the "centralization" provides zero benefit -- you can't reuse `DENO_LAND_BASE` outside this file, and the URLs still contain unique paths. This makes the file harder to read and grep for specific URLs.

### 14. `8c0a153cd` - use helper-built LoRA URLs in lora utils
**BAD.** Creates `buildHuggingFaceUrl(repo, path)` to construct `https://huggingface.co/{repo}/resolve/main/{path}`. This replaces 15 readable URLs with function calls that split the URL into two string arguments. The URLs are static constants -- there's no runtime variation, no validation, no benefit to decomposing them. The file grows from ~17 deletions to +76 additions (net +59 lines). Makes URLs harder to search for ("what LoRA points to `lightx2v/Wan2.2-Lightning`?") and impossible to copy-paste from the code into a browser. Pure negative value.

### 15. `e9fe925ce` - replace hardcoded url placeholder in edit panel
**GOOD.** Replaces a fake example URL placeholder (`https://huggingface.co/.../lora.safetensors`) with descriptive text (`Enter a Hugging Face LoRA URL`). Simple, correct, improves UX.

---

## Summary

| Commit | Verdict | Issue |
|--------|---------|-------|
| `4bf30b2c1` raf sticky observer | GOOD | Real dedup, 2 consumers, behavioral |
| `3daf9422c` join clips frame scaling | GOOD | Real dedup, pure function |
| `80ae51ff2` training dialogs + formatters | QUESTIONABLE | `ShotCardPlaceholderGrid` is a micro-extraction (5 divs) |
| `4f0248b10` stale passthrough findings | BAD | Empty commit, zero changes |
| `a6897d2c0` layout classes | QUESTIONABLE | Single-file constants used once each, no dedup |
| `b09413fbc` header section types | QUESTIONABLE | 2-consumer type sharing in same directory, borderline |
| `34252f189` edge auth guards | GOOD | 13+ consumers, consistent pattern |
| `9ca1eeaa5` travel UI + drag | QUESTIONABLE | `joinClipsFormProps.ts` is property-mapping boilerplate (lightboxRenderBuilders anti-pattern); kitchen-sink commit; some single-consumer extractions |
| `94ba594b6` settings inheritance | GOOD | Clean parameterized hook, real dedup |
| `d591317eb` unused imports | QUESTIONABLE | Re-exports dead code that's deleted 2 commits later; double-semicolon artifacts |
| `93c1abc29` debug log removal | BAD | Removes operational error logging from critical edge function, including a security audit log |
| `81d3da7a2` dead export removal | GOOD | Correctly narrows visibility, confirmed dead code |
| `b1cc99803` edge alias URLs | BAD | Pointless decomposition of static test config URLs |
| `8c0a153cd` LoRA URLs | BAD | Decomposes static URL constants into function calls, +59 net lines, hurts readability/searchability |
| `e9fe925ce` URL placeholder | GOOD | Simple UX improvement |

**Action items (in priority order):**
1. **Revert `93c1abc29`** -- restore the `console.warn` calls in `complete_task`. These are operational logs, not debug noise. The security warning in `validateStoragePathSecurity` is especially important.
2. **Revert `8c0a153cd`** -- the `buildHuggingFaceUrl` helper is negative value.
3. **Revert `b1cc99803`** -- the edge alias URL decomposition is negative value.
4. **Delete empty commit `4f0248b10`** (or squash it away).
5. **Review `9ca1eeaa5`** -- `joinClipsFormProps.ts` should be flagged as property-mapping boilerplate per your anti-pattern guidelines. Consider inlining it back.
6. Fix the `;;` double-semicolon artifacts from `d591317eb`.

diff --git a/src/shared/editMedia/EditMediaSelectionLayout.test.tsx b/src/shared/editMedia/EditMediaSelectionLayout.test.tsx

@@ -0,0 +1,54 @@
+import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
+import { ImageIcon } from 'lucide-react';
+import type React from 'react';
+
+import { EditMediaSelectionLayout } from './EditMediaSelectionLayout';
+
+type LayoutProps = React.ComponentProps<typeof EditMediaSelectionLayout>;
+
+function renderLayout(overrides: Partial<LayoutProps> = {}) {
+  const onFileUpload = vi.fn();
+  const baseProps: LayoutProps = {
+    isMobile: false,
+    isDraggingOver: false,
+    isUploading: false,
+    dropIcon: ImageIcon,
+    dropLabel: 'Drop file',
+    uploadLabel: 'Upload file',
+    uploadingLabel: 'Uploading...',
+    mobileHint: 'Tap to upload',
+    desktopHint: 'Drag and drop a file',
+    accept: 'image/*',
+    onFileUpload,
+    rightPanel: <div data-testid="right-panel">panel</div>,
+  };
+
+  const result = render(<EditMediaSelectionLayout {...baseProps} {...overrides} />);
+  return { ...result, onFileUpload };
+}
+
+describe('EditMediaSelectionLayout', () => {
+  it('renders desktop hint and upload button in default state', () => {
+    renderLayout();
+
+    expect(screen.getByText('Drag and drop a file')).toBeInTheDocument();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Upload file' })).toBeInTheDocument();
+    expect(screen.getByTestId('right-panel')).toBeInTheDocument();
+  });
+
+  it('renders mobile hint in mobile mode', () => {
+    renderLayout({ isMobile: true });
+    expect(screen.getByText('Tap to upload')).toBeInTheDocument();
+  });
+
+  it('renders drag overlay when dragging', () => {
+    renderLayout({ isDraggingOver: true });
+    expect(screen.getByText('Drop file')).toBeInTheDocument();
+  });
+
+  it('renders upload overlay when uploading', () => {
+    renderLayout({ isUploading: true });
+    expect(screen.getByText('Uploading...')).toBeInTheDocument();
+  });
+});
diff --git a/src/shared/editMedia/navigation.test.ts b/src/shared/editMedia/navigation.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..c07eb62fe
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/shared/editMedia/navigation.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,70 @@
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+
+const mocks = vi.hoisted(() => ({
+  fetchGenerationById: vi.fn(),
+  normalizeAndPresentError: vi.fn(),
+}));
+
+vi.mock('@/integrations/supabase/repositories/generationRepository', () => ({
+  fetchGenerationById: (...args: unknown[]) => mocks.fetchGenerationById(...args),
+}));
+
+vi.mock('@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError', () => ({
+  normalizeAndPresentError: (...args: unknown[]) => mocks.normalizeAndPresentError(...args),
+}));
+
+import { navigateToGenerationById } from './navigation';
+
+describe('editMedia/navigation navigateToGenerationById', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    vi.clearAllMocks();
+  });
+
+  it('resolves generation and calls callbacks', async () => {
+    const generation = { id: 'gen-1' };
+    const onResolved = vi.fn();
+    const onAfterResolved = vi.fn();
+    mocks.fetchGenerationById.mockResolvedValue(generation);
+
+    await navigateToGenerationById('gen-1', {
+      context: 'navigation.test',
+      onResolved,
+      onAfterResolved,
+    });
+
+    expect(onResolved).toHaveBeenCalledWith(generation);
+    expect(onAfterResolved).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    expect(mocks.normalizeAndPresentError).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+
+  it('does nothing when no generation is returned', async () => {
+    const onResolved = vi.fn();
+    const onAfterResolved = vi.fn();
+    mocks.fetchGenerationById.mockResolvedValue(null);
+
+    await navigateToGenerationById('missing-gen', {
+      context: 'navigation.test',
+      onResolved,
+      onAfterResolved,
+    });
+
+    expect(onResolved).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+    expect(onAfterResolved).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+
+  it('normalizes errors from repository lookup', async () => {
+    const onResolved = vi.fn();
+    mocks.fetchGenerationById.mockRejectedValue(new Error('boom'));
+
+    await navigateToGenerationById('gen-1', {
+      context: 'navigation.test',
+      onResolved,
+    });
+
+    expect(onResolved).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+    expect(mocks.normalizeAndPresentError).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
+      expect.any(Error),
+      { context: 'navigation.test', showToast: false },
+    );
+  });
+});

+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+
+const mocks = vi.hoisted(() => ({
+  resolveAuthenticatedMediaUserId: vi.fn(),
+}));
+
+vi.mock('@/shared/lib/media/videoThumbnailGenerator', () => ({
+  resolveAuthenticatedMediaUserId: (...args: unknown[]) => mocks.resolveAuthenticatedMediaUserId(...args),
+}));
+
+import { requireProjectAndUserId } from './uploadGuards';
+
+describe('editMedia/uploadGuards requireProjectAndUserId', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    vi.clearAllMocks();
+    mocks.resolveAuthenticatedMediaUserId.mockResolvedValue('user-1');
+  });
+
+  it('throws when project id is missing', async () => {
+    await expect(requireProjectAndUserId(undefined)).rejects.toThrow('No project selected');
+    expect(mocks.resolveAuthenticatedMediaUserId).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+
+  it('returns project and user ids when project id is provided', async () => {
+    await expect(requireProjectAndUserId('project-1')).resolves.toEqual({
+      projectId: 'project-1',
+      userId: 'user-1',
+    });
+    expect(mocks.resolveAuthenticatedMediaUserId).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+  });
+});
diff --git a/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/TimelineItem.helpers.test.ts b/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/TimelineItem.helpers.test.ts
new file mode 100644

@@ -0,0 +1,88 @@
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+
+const mocks = vi.hoisted(() => ({
+  getAspectRatioStyle: vi.fn(),
+}));
+
+vi.mock('@/shared/components/ShotImageManager/utils/image-utils', () => ({
+  getAspectRatioStyle: (...args: unknown[]) => mocks.getAspectRatioStyle(...args),
+}));
+
+import { TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET } from './constants';
+import { getTimelineItemAspectRatioStyle, getTimelineItemPosition } from './TimelineItem.helpers';
+
+describe('TimelineItem.helpers', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    vi.clearAllMocks();
+    mocks.getAspectRatioStyle.mockReturnValue({ aspectRatio: '16/9' });
+  });
+
+  describe('getTimelineItemAspectRatioStyle', () => {
+    it('uses metadata width and height when available', () => {
+      const style = getTimelineItemAspectRatioStyle(
+        { metadata: { width: 800, height: 400 } } as never,
+        '1:1',
+      );
+      expect(style).toEqual({ aspectRatio: '2' });
+      expect(mocks.getAspectRatioStyle).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+    });
+
+    it('falls back to resolution in original params', () => {
+      const style = getTimelineItemAspectRatioStyle(
+        {
+          metadata: {
+            originalParams: {
+              orchestrator_details: { resolution: '1920x1080' },
+            },
+          },
+        } as never,
+        '1:1',
+      );
+      expect(style).toEqual({ aspectRatio: `${1920 / 1080}` });
+    });
+
+    it('falls back to project ratio style when dimensions are missing', () => {
+      const style = getTimelineItemAspectRatioStyle({ metadata: {} } as never, '4:3');
+      expect(style).toEqual({ aspectRatio: '16/9' });
+      expect(mocks.getAspectRatioStyle).toHaveBeenCalledWith('4:3');
+    });
+  });
+
+  describe('getTimelineItemPosition', () => {
+    it('returns frame position in timeline coordinates', () => {
+      const result = getTimelineItemPosition({
+        timelineWidth: 1000,
+        fullMinFrames: 0,
+        fullRange: 100,
+        framePosition: 50,
+        isDragging: false,
+        dragOffset: null,
+        originalFramePos: 50,
+        currentDragFrame: null,
+      });
+
+      const effectiveWidth = 1000 - TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET * 2;
+      const expectedPixel = TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET + (50 / 100) * effectiveWidth;
+      expect(result.leftPercent).toBeCloseTo((expectedPixel / 1000) * 100, 6);
+      expect(result.displayFrame).toBe(50);
+    });
+
+    it('uses drag offset and current drag frame while dragging', () => {
+      const result = getTimelineItemPosition({
+        timelineWidth: 1000,
+        fullMinFrames: 0,
+        fullRange: 100,
+        framePosition: 60,
+        isDragging: true,
+        dragOffset: { x: 20, y: 0 },
+        originalFramePos: 40,
+        currentDragFrame: 42,
+      });
+
+      const effectiveWidth = 1000 - TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET * 2;
+      const originalPixel = TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET + (40 / 100) * effectiveWidth;
+      expect(result.leftPercent).toBeCloseTo(((originalPixel + 20) / 1000) * 100, 6);
+      expect(result.displayFrame).toBe(42);
+    });
+  });
+});

diff --git a/supabase/functions/complete_task/request.test.ts b/supabase/functions/complete_task/request.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..33803a80a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/complete_task/request.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,149 @@
+import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+
+import { parseCompleteTaskRequest, validateStoragePathSecurity } from './request.ts';
+
+function createTaskLookupClient(result: { data: { task_type: string } | null; error: { message: string } | null }) {
+  const single = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(result);
+  const eq = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ single });
+  const select = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ eq });
+  const from = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ select });
+
+  return {
+    client: { from },
+    spies: { from, select, eq, single },
+  };
+}
+
+describe('complete_task/request parseCompleteTaskRequest', () => {
+  it('rejects multipart uploads', async () => {
+    const request = new Request('https://edge.test/complete-task', {
+      method: 'POST',
+      headers: { 'content-type': 'multipart/form-data; boundary=abc' },
+      body: '--abc--',
+    });
+
+    const result = await parseCompleteTaskRequest(request);
+
+    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
+    if (!result.success) {
+      expect(result.response.status).toBe(400);
+      await expect(result.response.json()).resolves.toMatchObject({
+        errorCode: 'multipart_not_supported',
+      });
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('parses base64 mode payloads', async () => {
+    const request = new Request('https://edge.test/complete-task', {
+      method: 'POST',
+      headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
+      body: JSON.stringify({
+        task_id: 'task-1',
+        file_data: 'aGVsbG8=',
+        filename: 'output.png',
+      }),
+    });
+
+    const result = await parseCompleteTaskRequest(request);
+
+    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
+    if (result.success) {
+      expect(result.data.mode).toBe('base64');
+      expect(result.data.taskId).toBe('task-1');
+      expect(result.data.filename).toBe('output.png');
+      expect(result.data.fileData).toBeInstanceOf(Uint8Array);
+      expect(Array.from(result.data.fileData ?? [])).toEqual([104, 101, 108, 108, 111]);
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('parses presigned storage path payloads and marks orchestrator check when task ids differ', async () => {
+    const request = new Request('https://edge.test/complete-task', {
+      method: 'POST',
+      headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
+      body: JSON.stringify({
+        task_id: 'task-target',
+        storage_path: 'user-1/tasks/task-source/out.png',
+      }),
+    });
+
+    const result = await parseCompleteTaskRequest(request);
+
+    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
+    if (result.success) {
+      expect(result.data.mode).toBe('presigned');
+      expect(result.data.taskId).toBe('task-target');
+      expect(result.data.storagePathTaskId).toBe('task-source');
+      expect(result.data.requiresOrchestratorCheck).toBe(true);
+    }
+  });
+});
+
+describe('complete_task/request validateStoragePathSecurity', () => {
+  it('allows matching task ids without database lookup', async () => {
+    const lookup = createTaskLookupClient({
+      data: { task_type: 'image_generation' },
+      error: null,
+    });
+
+    const result = await validateStoragePathSecurity(
+      lookup.client as never,
+      'task-1',
+      'user-1/tasks/task-1/out.png',
+      'task-1',
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({ allowed: true });
+    expect(lookup.spies.from).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+
+  it('allows mismatched task ids for orchestrator tasks', async () => {
+    const lookup = createTaskLookupClient({
+      data: { task_type: 'image_orchestrator' },
+      error: null,
+    });
+
+    const result = await validateStoragePathSecurity(
+      lookup.client as never,
+      'task-target',
+      'user-1/tasks/task-source/out.png',
+      'task-source',
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({ allowed: true });
+    expect(lookup.spies.from).toHaveBeenCalledWith('tasks');
+  });
+
+  it('rejects mismatched task ids for non-orchestrator tasks', async () => {
+    const lookup = createTaskLookupClient({
+      data: { task_type: 'image_generation' },
+      error: null,
+    });
+
+    const result = await validateStoragePathSecurity(
+      lookup.client as never,
+      'task-target',
+      'user-1/tasks/task-source/out.png',
+      'task-source',
+    );
+
+    expect(result.allowed).toBe(false);
+    expect(result.error).toContain('storage_path does not match task_id');
+  });
+
+  it('rejects when task lookup fails', async () => {
+    const lookup = createTaskLookupClient({
+      data: null,
+      error: { message: 'db unavailable' },
+    });
+
+    const result = await validateStoragePathSecurity(
+      lookup.client as never,
+      'task-target',
+      'user-1/tasks/task-source/out.png',
+      'task-source',
+    );
+
+    expect(result.allowed).toBe(false);
+    expect(result.error).toContain('storage_path does not match task_id');
+  });
+});

OK, the function is `void` and there's nothing after the `if (error)` block inside the `try` -- the `if` block is the last statement before the `catch`. Here is my full assessment of all four commits:

---

## 1. `576e45309` — refactor(edit-tools): decouple shared edit-media helpers

**Verdict: GOOD**

The move from `src/tools/shared/editMedia/` to `src/shared/editMedia/` is correct. These helpers (`EditMediaSelectionLayout`, `navigation`, `uploadGuards`) are used by both `edit-images` and `edit-video` tools, so they belong in the shared layer, not under `src/tools/shared/`. The import updates in `EditImagesPage.tsx` and `EditVideoPage.tsx` are consistent. No stale references remain to the old `tools/shared/editMedia` path or to the deleted `requireAuthenticatedUserId.ts` re-export shim.

One note: `uploadGuards.ts` switched from importing `requireAuthenticatedUserId` (a re-export alias) to importing `resolveAuthenticatedMediaUserId` directly from `videoThumbnailGenerator.ts`. This is fine -- it eliminates a pointless indirection file. The deleted `requireAuthenticatedUserId.ts` was a single-line re-export, so removing it is a net improvement.

No issues found.

---

## 2. `0d7cc15b2` — test(complete-task): add direct request parsing coverage

**Verdict: GOOD**

Tests real behavior, not slop. Specifically:

- Tests that multipart uploads are rejected with the correct error code (`multipart_not_supported`).
- Tests base64 decoding: verifies the actual decoded bytes `[104, 101, 108, 108, 111]` match `"hello"`.
- Tests presigned storage path parsing: verifies `storagePathTaskId` extraction and `requiresOrchestratorCheck` flag logic.
- Tests `validateStoragePathSecurity` across four scenarios: matching IDs (skips DB), orchestrator task (allowed), non-orchestrator mismatch (rejected), and DB failure (rejected).

The mock client builder (`createTaskLookupClient`) correctly chains `.from().select().eq().single()` matching Supabase's fluent API. Tests are well-structured and verify security-critical behavior.

No issues found.

---

## 3. `24a11309a` — test(queue): add direct coverage for shared editMedia and timeline helpers

**Verdict: GOOD, with one minor note**

**Tests:** All four test files test real behavior:
- `EditMediaSelectionLayout.test.tsx` -- renders the component in four states (desktop, mobile, dragging, uploading) and checks the right text appears. Straightforward component test.
- `navigation.test.ts` -- tests `navigateToGenerationById` with three paths: success, null generation, and error. Verifies callbacks are invoked correctly and errors are normalized.
- `uploadGuards.test.ts` -- tests `requireProjectAndUserId` throws on missing projectId and returns correct shape otherwise.
- `TimelineItem.helpers.test.ts` -- tests position calculation with and without drag state, and aspect ratio style resolution through three fallback levels (metadata dimensions, resolution string, project ratio). This tests actual math including `TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET`.

**Alias file change (`vitest.edge.aliases.ts`):** The refactor compresses the alias map from ~49 lines to ~23 lines. The resolved URLs are identical:
- `DENO_STD` = `https://deno.land/std` (same as before)
- `ESM` = `https://esm.sh` (same as before)
- `NPM` = `npm:` (same as before)

The URLs remain readable via the template literal keys. The `mockPath` helper is a trivial wrapper around `path.resolve` -- marginally less readable than the explicit calls, but the reduction in line count is worth it.

No bugs. The only minor nit is that `mockPath` doesn't add much value over inlining `path.resolve`, but it's harmless.

---

## 4. `cff7579b0` — chore(complete-task): add warning logs on recoverable failures

**Verdict: GOOD, with one nit**

All log additions are in the right places and use the right level (`console.warn`). These are all recoverable failure paths that previously swallowed errors silently:

- `generation-child.ts:131` -- warns when variant creation returns falsy before falling through to new generation. Good: this is a surprising code path that should be visible.
- `generation-core.ts:117,126` -- warns on error/exception finding source generation (returns null either way).
- `generation-core.ts:212,215` -- warns on error/exception linking generation to shot.
- `request.ts:157,168` -- warns on base64 decode failures (main file and thumbnail).
- `request.ts:249,258` -- warns on storage path security validation failures.
- `storage.ts:73,117,121,215,230` -- warns on upload errors, thumbnail errors, file verification errors, and cleanup failures.

**One nit (not a bug):** In `linkGenerationToShot`, the original code had `return;` inside `if (error)`. The new code removed that `return` but kept it in the `catch` block. Since the `if (error)` block is the last statement in the `try`, this has no behavioral impact -- but it's an inconsistency. The `return` should either be kept in both places or removed from both.

**Log levels are appropriate:** `console.warn` is correct for all of these -- they're recoverable failures that shouldn't crash the function but should be visible in logs for debugging.

---

| Commit | Verdict | Notes |
|--------|---------|-------|
| `576e45309` refactor | **GOOD** | Clean file move, no stale refs, removes indirection shim |
| `0d7cc15b2` test request parsing | **GOOD** | Tests real security-critical behavior |
| `24a11309a` test editMedia + timeline | **GOOD** | Real behavior tests; alias cleanup is correct |
| `cff7579b0` warning logs | **GOOD** | Right places, right levels; minor `return` inconsistency in `linkGenerationToShot` (cosmetic only) |
